Materials go into `__constant__` memory (broadcast reads).\n- **`simulationKernel`**: one thread per element (grid-stride loop), gathers neighbour energies through the read-only cache (`__ldg`), and accumulates flux in the exact same order as the reference loop so the arithmetic is bit-identical.\n- **Buffering**: energy uses ping-pong device buffers (pointer swap, no copy); `total_flux` is accumulated in place in a single array since each thread only touches its own entry — this is equivalent to the original's per-iteration copy through the swap buffer and removes read/write traffic.\n- **Timing**: mesh upload/allocation happens in the setup phase (alongside mesh building, with CUDA context forced up front); the timed `runSimulation` covers the kernel launches, the synchronizing device→host copy, and the write-back into `world.elements_dynamic`, so validation/hash/`-r` output work unchanged.\n- **CMake**: project now enables the CUDA language, compiles `unstructured.cpp` as CUDA with `-O3` (host flags passed through `-Xcompiler`), `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`.
